Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on computing structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 252. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 868 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by computing generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 252, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 868. QED.
